/bin/bash -lc "python - <<'PY'
import importlib.util, math
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from itertools import product
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('solver','/app/submissions/solve.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
inst=mod.Instance()

def best_pattern(order_id, station_id, penalties):
    lines=sorted(inst.order_lines[order_id], key=lambda r:(len(inst.line_candidates[(order_id,r['sku_id'])]), r['sku_id']))
    best=None; best_key=None
    for ass in product(*[inst.line_candidates[(order_id,r['sku_id'])] for r in lines]):
        used=sorted(set(ass))
        if len(used)>int(inst.orders[order_id]['max_split_pods']):
            continue
        bypod=defaultdict(list); usage=Counter()
        for r,p in zip(lines,ass):
            bypod[p].append((r['sku_id'], r['quantity']))
            usage[(p,r['sku_id'])]+=r['quantity']
        visits=[]
        for p,items in bypod.items():
            visits.extend(mod.split_items_into_visits(inst,station_id,p,items,inst.orders[order_id]['hazmat']=='1'))
        travel=sum(v.travel_distance for v in visits)
        energy=sum(v.travel_distance*float(inst.robots[next(iter(inst.robots))]['energy_per_distance']) for v in visits)
        totaldur=sum(v.duration for v in visits)
        pen=sum(penalties.get(v.pod_id,0.0)*v.duration for v in visits)
        cost=inst.weights['split_order']*(len(used)-1)+inst.weights['visit_travel']*travel+inst.weights['energy_used']*energy+pen
        key=(cost,totaldur,len(visits),tuple(used))
        if best_key is None or key<best_key:
            best_key=key
            best=dict(station_id=station_id,line_to_pod=tuple(sorted((r['sku_id'],p) for r,p in zip(lines,ass))),visits=tuple(visits),local_cost=cost,total_duration=totaldur,usage_by_pod_sku=tuple(sorted((p,sku,qty) for (p,sku),qty in usage.items())))
    return best

def solve_master(patterns,mult=4.0):
    model=Model('m'); mod.configure_scip(model,15)
    y={}
    for o,plist in patterns.items():
        for i,p in enumerate(plist): y[o,i]=model.addVar(vtype='B')
    pairs=[('ST_A','ST_B'),('ST_A','ST_C'),('ST_B','ST_C')]
    load={s:quicksum(p['total_duration']*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,p in enumerate(plist) if p['station_id']==s) for s in inst.stations}
    diff={pair:model.addVar(lb=0) for pair in pairs}
    for o,plist in patterns.items(): model.addCons(quicksum(y[o,i] for i in range(len(plist)))==1)
    keys=set()
    for plist in patterns.values():
        for p in plist:
            for pod,sku,qty in p['usage_by_pod_sku']: keys.add((pod,sku))
    for pod,sku in keys:
        expr=quicksum(qty*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,p in enumerate(plist) for p2,sku2,qty in p['usage_by_pod_sku'] if p2==pod and sku2==sku)
        model.addCons(expr<=inst.pod_inventory[pod].get(sku,0))
    for s1,s2 in pairs:
        model.addCons(diff[s1,s2]>=load[s1]-load[s2]); model.addCons(diff[s1,s2]>=load[s2]-load[s1])
    obj=quicksum(p['local_cost']*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,p in enumerate(plist))+mult*inst.weights['station_imbalance']*quicksum(diff[p] for p in pairs)
    model.setObjective(obj,'minimize'); model.optimize()
    sel={o: plist[max(range(len(plist)), key=lambda i:model.getVal(y[o,i]))] for o,plist in patterns.items()}
    return sel

def batch_schedule(sel):
    linepod={}; station_orders=defaultdict(list)
    for oid,p in sel.items():
        mp=dict(p['line_to_pod']); station_orders[p['station_id']].append(oid)
        for sku,pod in mp.items(): linepod[(oid,sku)]=pod
    for st in station_orders:
        station_orders[st].sort(key=lambda oid:(inst.orders[oid]['priority']"'!='"'priority', int(inst.orders[oid]['due_slot']), int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot']), oid))
    remaining={(oid,l['sku_id']): l['quantity'] for oid,lines in inst.order_lines.items() for l in lines}
    active_cap={st:min(cap for _,_,cap in inst.station_windows[st]) for st in inst.stations}
    active={st:[] for st in inst.stations}; pending={st:list(v) for st,v in station_orders.items()}
    lane_avail={st:[0]*int(inst.stations[st]['pod_visits_per_slot']) for st in inst.stations}
    robot_states={rb: mod.RobotState(int(r['shift_start']),'zone',r['start_zone'],float(r['initial_battery']),False) for rb,r in inst.robots.items()}
    pod_avail={p:0 for p in inst.pods}; aisle_usage=Counter(); completion={}; visits=[]
    def fill(st, now):
        while len(active[st])<active_cap[st]:
            idx=None
            for i,oid in enumerate(pending[st]):
                if int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot'])<=now: idx=i; break
            if idx is None: break
            active[st].append(pending[st].pop(idx))
    def next_release(st):
        return min([int(inst.orders[o]['release_slot']) for o in pending[st]], default=None)
    def candidate(st):
        groups=defaultdict(list)
        for oid in active[st]:
            for l in inst.order_lines[oid]:
                sku=l['sku_id']; rem=remaining[(oid,sku)]
                if rem>0: groups[linepod[(oid,sku)]].append((oid,sku,rem))
        best=None
        maxu=int(inst.stations[st]['max_units_per_visit'])
        for pod,items in groups.items():
            exp=[]
            for oid,sku,rem in items: exp.extend([(oid,sku)]*rem)
            exp.sort(key=lambda x:(inst.orders[x[0]]['priority']"'!='"'priority', int(inst.orders[x[0]]['due_slot']), x[0], x[1]))
            chunk=exp[:maxu]
            if not chunk: continue
            c=Counter(chunk)
            pick_pairs=tuple(sorted((oid,sku,qty) for (oid,sku),qty in c.items()))
            sku_counts=Counter(); haz=False; touched=set()
            for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs:
                sku_counts[sku]+=qty; touched.add(oid); haz = haz or inst.orders[oid]['hazmat']=='1'
            service=sum(q*int(inst.skus[s]['unit_pick_time']) for s,q in sku_counts.items())
            vt=mod.VisitTemplate(pod_id=pod,picks=tuple(sorted(sku_counts.items())),units=sum(sku_counts.values()),service_minutes=service,travel_distance=2*inst.loaded_travel_distance(pod,st),travel_slots=inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st),duration=2*inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st)+max(1,math.ceil(service/inst.slot_minutes)),hazmat=haz,aisle_group=inst.zones[inst.pods[pod]['zone_id']]['aisle_group'])
            urg=sum((1000 if inst.orders[oid]['priority']=='priority' else 0)+max(0,120-int(inst.orders[oid]['due_slot'])) for oid in touched)
            comp_bonus=0
            for oid in touched:
                total=sum(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])] for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
                taken=sum(q for o,s,q in pick_pairs if o==oid)
                if taken==total: comp_bonus += 100
            score=(comp_bonus+urg+8*len(touched)+sum(q for _,_,q in pick_pairs)-vt.duration, comp_bonus, urg)
            if best is None or score>best[0]: best=(score,pick_pairs,vt)
        return best
    steps=0
    while True:
        steps+=1
        if steps>5000: return None,None,None,None,'step_limit'
        options=[]; future=[]; any_pending=False
        for st in inst.stations:
            active[st]=[oid for oid in active[st] if any(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]>0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid])]
            now=min(lane_avail[st]); fill(st,now)
            if active[st] or pending[st]: any_pending=True
            if active[st]:
                lane=min(range(len(lane_avail[st])), key=lambda i: lane_avail[st][i])
                cand=candidate(st)
                if cand:
                    score,pick_pairs,vt=cand
                    found=mod.find_earliest_visit_start(inst,vt,st,lane_avail[st][lane],pod_avail,aisle_usage,robot_states)
                    if found: options.append((found[0], int(min(inst.orders[o]['due_slot'] for o in active[st])), st, lane, pick_pairs, vt, found[1]))
            else:
                nr=next_release(st)
                if nr is not None: future.append((nr,st))
        if not any_pending: break
        if not options:
            if not future: return None,None,None,None,'no_options'
            nr,st=min(future)
            lane=min(range(len(lane_avail[st])), key=lambda i: lane_avail[st][i])
            lane_avail[st][lane]=max(lane_avail[st][lane],nr)
            continue
        start,_,st,lane,pick_pairs,vt,rb=min(options)
        end=start+vt.duration
        for tau in range(start,end): aisle_usage[(vt.aisle_group,tau)] += 1
        pod_avail[vt.pod_id]=end+int(inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['return_buffer_slots'])
        rs=robot_states[rb]; energy=inst.energy_for_visit(rb,vt.pod_id,st)
        rs.available_time=end; rs.location_type='zone'; rs.location_id=inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['zone_id']; rs.battery-=energy; rs.last_visit_hazmat=vt.hazmat
        lane_avail[st][lane]=end; visits.append((st,vt.pod_id,start,end,rb,pick_pairs,vt))
        touched=set()
        for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs: remaining[(oid,sku)]-=qty; touched.add(oid)
        for oid in touched:
            if all(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]<=0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid]): completion[oid]=end
    rem=sum(max(0,v) for v in remaining.values())
    return visits,completion,lane_avail,remaining,rem

penalties={}
for it in range(6):
    patterns={oid:[best_pattern(oid,st,penalties) for st in inst.eligible_stations(oid)] for oid in inst.orders}
    sel=solve_master(patterns,4.0)
    visits,completion,lane_avail,remaining,rem=batch_schedule(sel)
    # pod block usage by batched schedule assignment even if rem>0 maybe partial; also static aggregate from assignment batching
    agg=defaultdict(list)
    for oid,p in sel.items():
        mp=dict(p['line_to_pod'])

        maxu=int(inst.stations[st]['max_units_per_visit'])
        exp=[]
        for oid,sku,q in items: exp.extend([(oid,sku)]*q)
        exp.sort(key=lambda x:(int(inst.orders[x[0]]['due_slot']),x[0],x[1]))
        while exp:
            chunk=exp[:maxu]; exp=exp[maxu:]
            sc=Counter(sku for oid,sku in chunk)
            serv=sum(q*int(inst.skus[s]['unit_pick_time']) for s,q in sc.items())
            dur=2*inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st)+max(1,math.ceil(serv/inst.slot_minutes))
            podblock[pod]+=dur+int(inst.pods[pod]['return_buffer_slots'])
    print('iter',it,'rem',rem,'max lane',{s:max(v) for s,v in lane_avail.items()} if lane_avail else None,'top podblock',podblock.most_common(8))
    if rem==0:
        print('feasible maybe, max comp',max(completion.values()))
        break
    # update penalties for overloaded / top pods
    for pod,val in podblock.items():
        if val>inst.horizon*0.85:
            penalties[pod]=penalties.get(pod,0)+8.0*(val-inst.horizon*0.85)
        elif val>inst.horizon*0.7:
            penalties[pod]=penalties.get(pod,0)+2.0*(val-inst.horizon*0.7)
    print('penalties',sorted((k,round(v,1)) for k,v in penalties.items())[:10], '... count',len(penalties))

def best_pattern(order_id, station_id):
    lines=sorted(inst.order_lines[order_id], key=lambda r:(len(inst.line_candidates[(order_id,r['sku_id'])]), r['sku_id']))
    best=None; best_key=None
    for ass in product(*[inst.line_candidates[(order_id,r['sku_id'])] for r in lines]):

        if len(used)>int(inst.orders[order_id]['max_split_pods']): continue

        key=(cost,sum(v.duration for v in visits),len(visits),tuple(used))

def fill(st, now):
    while len(active[st])<active_cap[st]:
        idx=None
        for i,oid in enumerate(pending[st]):
            if int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot'])<=now:
                idx=i; break
        if idx is None: break
        active[st].append(pending[st].pop(idx))

def next_release(st):
    return min([int(inst.orders[o]['release_slot']) for o in pending[st]], default=None)

def cand(st):
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for oid in active[st]:
        for l in inst.order_lines[oid]:
            sku=l['sku_id']; rem=remaining[(oid,sku)]
            if rem>0: groups[linepod[(oid,sku)]].append((oid,sku,rem))
    best=None
    maxu=int(inst.stations[st]['max_units_per_visit'])
    for pod,items in groups.items():
        exp=[]
        for oid,sku,rem in items: exp.extend([(oid,sku)]*rem)
        exp.sort(key=lambda x:(inst.orders[x[0]]['priority']"'!='"'priority', int(inst.orders[x[0]]['due_slot']), x[0], x[1]))
        chunk=exp[:maxu]
        if not chunk: continue
        c=Counter(chunk)
        pick_pairs=tuple(sorted((oid,sku,qty) for (oid,sku),qty in c.items()))
        sku_counts=Counter(); haz=False; touched=set()
        for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs:
            sku_counts[sku]+=qty; touched.add(oid); haz = haz or inst.orders[oid]['hazmat']=='1'
        service=sum(q*int(inst.skus[s]['unit_pick_time']) for s,q in sku_counts.items())
        vt=mod.VisitTemplate(pod_id=pod,picks=tuple(sorted(sku_counts.items())),units=sum(sku_counts.values()),service_minutes=service,travel_distance=2*inst.loaded_travel_distance(pod,st),travel_slots=inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st),duration=2*inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st)+max(1,math.ceil(service/inst.slot_minutes)),hazmat=haz,aisle_group=inst.zones[inst.pods[pod]['zone_id']]['aisle_group'])
        urg=sum((1000 if inst.orders[oid]['priority']=='priority' else 0)+max(0,120-int(inst.orders[oid]['due_slot'])) for oid in touched)
        comp_bonus=0
        for oid in touched:
            total=sum(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])] for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
            taken=sum(q for o,s,q in pick_pairs if o==oid)
            if taken==total: comp_bonus += 100
        score=(comp_bonus+urg+8*len(touched)+sum(q for _,_,q in pick_pairs)-vt.duration, comp_bonus, urg)
        if best is None or score>best[0]: best=(score,pick_pairs,vt)
    return best

for step in range(5000):
    options=[]; future=[]; any_pending=False
    for st in inst.stations:
        active[st]=[oid for oid in active[st] if any(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]>0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid])]
        now=min(lane_avail[st]); fill(st,now)
        if active[st] or pending[st]: any_pending=True
        if active[st]:
            lane=min(range(len(lane_avail[st])), key=lambda i: lane_avail[st][i])
            cc=cand(st)
            if cc:
                score,pick_pairs,vt=cc
                found=mod.find_earliest_visit_start(inst,vt,st,lane_avail[st][lane],pod_avail,aisle_usage,robot_states)
                if found: options.append((found[0], st, lane, pick_pairs, vt, found[1]))
        else:
            nr=next_release(st)
            if nr is not None: future.append((nr,st))
    if not any_pending:
        print('done'); break
    if not options:
        print('stuck at step',step,'future',future)
        for st in inst.stations:
            print('station',st,'lane',lane_avail[st],'active',active[st],'pending',pending[st][:5])
        break
    start,st,lane,pick_pairs,vt,rb=min(options)
    end=start+vt.duration
    for tau in range(start,end): aisle_usage[(vt.aisle_group,tau)] += 1
    pod_avail[vt.pod_id]=end+int(inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['return_buffer_slots'])
    rs=robot_states[rb]; energy=inst.energy_for_visit(rb,vt.pod_id,st)
    rs.available_time=end; rs.location_type='zone'; rs.location_id=inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['zone_id']; rs.battery-=energy; rs.last_visit_hazmat=vt.hazmat
    lane_avail[st][lane]=end; visits.append((st,vt.pod_id,start,end,rb,pick_pairs))

    for oid in list(active[st]):
        if all(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]<=0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid]):
            completion[oid]=end; active[st].remove(oid)
print('remaining units',sum(max(0,v) for v in remaining.values()))
for oid in sorted(inst.orders):
    rr=sum(max(0,remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]) for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
    if rr: print('remain',oid,rr,'station',sel[oid]['station_id'],dict(sel[oid]['line_to_pod']))
print('lane_avail',lane_avail)
print('robot battery',{rb:round(rs.battery,1) for rb,rs in robot_states.items()})

    return best

def fill_active(st, now):
    changed=False
    while len(active[st]) < active_cap[st]:
        idx=None
        for i,oid in enumerate(pending[st]):
            if int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot']) <= now:
                idx=i; break
        if idx is None: break
        active[st].append(pending[st].pop(idx)); changed=True
    return changed

def next_release(st):
    vals=[int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot']) for oid in pending[st]]
    return min(vals) if vals else None

def build_candidate(st):
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for oid in active[st]:
        for line in inst.order_lines[oid]:
            sku=line['sku_id']; rem=remaining[(oid,sku)]
            if rem<=0: continue
            pod=linepod[(oid,sku)]
            groups[pod].append((oid,sku,rem))
    if not groups: return None
    maxu=int(inst.stations[st]['max_units_per_visit'])
    best=None
    for pod,items in groups.items():
        expanded=[]
        for oid,sku,rem in items:
            expanded.extend([(oid,sku)]*rem)
        expanded.sort(key=lambda x:(inst.orders[x[0]]['priority']"'!='"'priority', int(inst.orders[x[0]]['due_slot']), x[0], x[1]))
        chunk=expanded[:maxu]
        c=Counter(chunk)
        pick_pairs=tuple(sorted((oid,sku,qty) for (oid,sku),qty in c.items()))
        sku_counts=Counter(); touched=set(); haz=False
        for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs:
            sku_counts[sku]+=qty; touched.add(oid); haz = haz or inst.orders[oid]['hazmat']=='1'
        service=sum(q*int(inst.skus[s]['unit_pick_time']) for s,q in sku_counts.items())
        dur=2*inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st)+max(1,math.ceil(service/inst.slot_minutes))
        vt=mod.VisitTemplate(pod_id=pod,picks=tuple(sorted(sku_counts.items())),units=sum(sku_counts.values()),service_minutes=service,travel_distance=2*inst.loaded_travel_distance(pod,st),travel_slots=inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st),duration=dur,hazmat=haz,aisle_group=inst.zones[inst.pods[pod]['zone_id']]['aisle_group'])
        urg=sum((1000 if inst.orders[oid]['priority']=='priority' else 0)+max(0,120-int(inst.orders[oid]['due_slot'])) for oid in touched)
        completion_bonus=0
        for oid in touched:
            total=sum(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])] for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
            taken=sum(q for o,s,q in pick_pairs if o==oid)
            if taken==total: completion_bonus += 100
        score=(completion_bonus + urg + 8*len(touched)+sum(q for _,_,q in pick_pairs)-dur, completion_bonus, urg)
        if best is None or score>best[0]: best=(score,pod,pick_pairs,vt)
    return best

steps=0
while True:
    steps += 1
    if steps>5000:
        print('too many steps'); break
    any_pending=False; station_options=[]; future=[]
    for st in inst.stations:
        active[st]=[oid for oid in active[st] if any(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]>0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid])]

        lane_avail[st][lane]=max(lane_avail[st][lane], nr)
        continue

    pod_avail[pod]=end+int(inst.pods[pod]['return_buffer_slots'])

    rs.available_time=end; rs.location_type='zone'; rs.location_id=inst.pods[pod]['zone_id']; rs.battery-=energy; rs.last_visit_hazmat=vt.hazmat

for o,plist in patterns.items(): model.addCons(quicksum(y[o,i] for i in range(len(plist)))==1)
keys=set((p,sku) for plist in patterns.values() for pat in plist for p,sku,_ in pat['usage_by_pod_sku'])
for p,sku in keys:
    model.addCons(quicksum(q*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,pat in enumerate(plist) for p2,sku2,q in pat['usage_by_pod_sku'] if p2==p and sku2==sku)<=inst.pod_inventory[p].get(sku,0))
pairs=[('ST_A','ST_B'),('ST_A','ST_C'),('ST_B','ST_C')]
load={s:quicksum(pat['total_duration']*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,pat in enumerate(plist) if pat['station_id']==s) for s in inst.stations}
diff={pair:model.addVar(lb=0) for pair in pairs}
for s1,s2 in pairs: model.addCons(diff[s1,s2]>=load[s1]-load[s2]); model.addCons(diff[s1,s2]>=load[s2]-load[s1])
obj=quicksum(pat['local_cost']*y[o,i] for o,plist in patterns.items() for i,pat in enumerate(plist))+4.0*inst.weights['station_imbalance']*quicksum(diff[p] for p in pairs)
model.setObjective(obj,'minimize'); model.optimize()
sel={o: plist[max(range(len(plist)), key=lambda i:model.getVal(y[o,i]))] for o,plist in patterns.items()}

active_cap={st:min(cap for _,_,cap in inst.station_windows[st]) for st in inst.stations}
active={st:[] for st in inst.stations}; pending={st:list(v) for st,v in station_orders.items()}
lane_avail={st:[0]*int(inst.stations[st]['pod_visits_per_slot']) for st in inst.stations}
robot_states={rb: mod.RobotState(int(r['shift_start']),'zone',r['start_zone'],float(r['initial_battery']),False) for rb,r in inst.robots.items()}
pod_avail={p:0 for p in inst.pods}; aisle_usage=Counter(); order_completion={}

def fill(st, now):
    while len(active[st])<active_cap[st]:
        idx=None
        for i,oid in enumerate(pending[st]):
            if int(inst.orders[oid]['release_slot'])<=now: idx=i; break
        if idx is None: break
        active[st].append(pending[st].pop(idx))
def next_release(st):
    return min([int(inst.orders[o]['release_slot']) for o in pending[st]], default=None)
def cand(st):
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for oid in active[st]:
        for l in inst.order_lines[oid]:
            rem=remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]
            if rem>0: groups[linepod[(oid,l['sku_id'])]].append((oid,l['sku_id'],rem))
    maxu=int(inst.stations[st]['max_units_per_visit']); best=None
    for pod,items in groups.items():
        exp=[]
        for oid,sku,rem in items: exp.extend([(oid,sku)]*rem)
        exp.sort(key=lambda x:(inst.orders[x[0]]['priority']"'!='"'priority', int(inst.orders[x[0]]['due_slot']), x[0], x[1]))
        chunk=exp[:maxu]
        if not chunk: continue
        c=Counter(chunk); pick_pairs=tuple(sorted((oid,sku,qty) for (oid,sku),qty in c.items()))
        sku_counts=Counter(); touched=set(); haz=False
        for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs:
            sku_counts[sku]+=qty; touched.add(oid); haz = haz or inst.orders[oid]['hazmat']=='1'
        service=sum(q*int(inst.skus[s]['unit_pick_time']) for s,q in sku_counts.items())
        vt=mod.VisitTemplate(pod_id=pod,picks=tuple(sorted(sku_counts.items())),units=sum(sku_counts.values()),service_minutes=service,travel_distance=2*inst.loaded_travel_distance(pod,st),travel_slots=inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st),duration=2*inst.loaded_travel_slots(pod,st)+max(1,math.ceil(service/inst.slot_minutes)),hazmat=haz,aisle_group=inst.zones[inst.pods[pod]['zone_id']]['aisle_group'])
        due=min(int(inst.orders[oid]['due_slot']) for oid in touched)
        comp_bonus=0
        for oid in touched:
            total=sum(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])] for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
            taken=sum(q for o,s,q in pick_pairs if o==oid)
            if taken==total: comp_bonus += 100
        score=(due, -comp_bonus, vt.duration)
        if best is None or score<best[0]: best=(score,pick_pairs,vt,touched)
    return best
for step in range(5000):
    options=[]; future=[]; any_pending=False
    for st in inst.stations:
        active[st]=[oid for oid in active[st] if any(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]>0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid])]
        now=min(lane_avail[st]); fill(st,now)
        if active[st] or pending[st]: any_pending=True
        if active[st]:
            lane=min(range(len(lane_avail[st])), key=lambda i: lane_avail[st][i])
            cc=cand(st)
            if cc:
                _,pick_pairs,vt,touched=cc
                found=mod.find_earliest_visit_start(inst,vt,st,lane_avail[st][lane],pod_avail,aisle_usage,robot_states)
                if found:
                    min_due=min(int(inst.orders[o]['due_slot']) for o in touched)
                    slack=min_due-(found[0]+vt.duration)
                    options.append((slack, found[0], min_due, st, lane, pick_pairs, vt, found[1]))
        else:
            nr=next_release(st)
            if nr is not None: future.append((nr,st))
    if not any_pending: break
    if not options:
        if not future:
            print('stuck'); break
        nr,st=min(future)
        lane=min(range(len(lane_avail[st])), key=lambda i: lane_avail[st][i])
        lane_avail[st][lane]=max(lane_avail[st][lane],nr); continue
    slack,start,min_due,st,lane,pick_pairs,vt,rb=min(options)
    end=start+vt.duration
    for tau in range(start,end): aisle_usage[(vt.aisle_group,tau)] += 1
    pod_avail[vt.pod_id]=end+int(inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['return_buffer_slots'])

    rs.available_time=end; rs.location_type='zone'; rs.location_id=inst.pods[vt.pod_id]['zone_id']; rs.battery-=energy; rs.last_visit_hazmat=vt.hazmat
    lane_avail[st][lane]=end
    for oid,sku,qty in pick_pairs: remaining[(oid,sku)]-=qty
    for oid in list(active[st]):
        if all(remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]<=0 for l in inst.order_lines[oid]): order_completion[oid]=end; active[st].remove(oid)
rem=sum(max(0,v) for v in remaining.values())
print('remaining',rem)
for oid in sorted(inst.orders):
    rr=sum(max(0,remaining[(oid,l['sku_id'])]) for l in inst.order_lines[oid])
    if rr: print('remain',oid,rr)
print('lane_avail',lane_avail)
print('batt',{rb:round(rs.battery,1) for rb,rs in robot_states.items()})
